User: What is 24 * 15?
Joe: 24 * 15 = 24 * 10 + 24 * 5 = 240 + 120 = 360.
User: What is 17 + 38 + 45?
Joe: 17 + 38 = 55, 55 + 45 = 100, so 17 + 38 + 45 = 100.
User: What is 144 / 12?
Joe: 12 * 12 = 144, so 144 / 12 = 12.
User: What is 9 * 9 * 3?
Joe: 9 * 9 = 81, 81 * 3 = 243, so 9 * 9 * 3 = 243.
User: What is 256 - 189?
Joe: 256 - 189 = 256 - 200 + 11 = 56 + 11 = 67.
User: If x + 7 = 23, what is x?
Joe: x + 7 = 23, so x = 23 - 7 = 16.
User: If 3y = 45, what is y?
Joe: 3y = 45, so y = 45 / 3 = 15.
User: If 2x + 5 = 19, what is x?
Joe: 2x + 5 = 19, so 2x = 14, so x = 14 / 2 = 7.
User: If 4n - 3 = 13, what is n?
Joe: 4n - 3 = 13, so 4n = 16, so n = 16 / 4 = 4.
User: If x/3 + 2 = 7, what is x?
Joe: x/3 + 2 = 7, so x/3 = 5, so x = 15.
User: A shirt costs $40 and is on sale for 25% off. What is the sale price?
Joe: 25% of 40 = 40 * 0.25 = 10, so sale price = 40 - 10 = 30. The sale price is $30.
User: You buy 3 books at $12 each with a $5 coupon. What do you pay?
Joe: 3 * 12 = 36, then 36 - 5 = 31. You pay $31.
User: A meal costs $24 and you leave a 20% tip. What is the total?
Joe: 20% of 24 = 24 * 0.20 = 4.80, so total = 24 + 4.80 = $28.80.
User: 15% of what number is 45?
Joe: 15% of x = 45, so 0.15x = 45, so x = 45 / 0.15 = 300.
User: What is 3/4 + 1/6?
Joe: LCD of 4 and 6 is 12, so 3/4 = 9/12 and 1/6 = 2/12, so 9/12 + 2/12 = 11/12.
User: What is 5/8 - 1/4?
Joe: 1/4 = 2/8, so 5/8 - 2/8 = 3/8.
User: What is 2/3 * 9/10?
Joe: 2/3 * 9/10 = (2 * 9) / (3 * 10) = 18/30 = 3/5.
User: What is (3/4) / (2/5)?
Joe: (3/4) / (2/5) = 3/4 * 5/2 = 15/8 = 1 7/8.
User: What is 7 * 8 + 6 * 3?
Joe: 7 * 8 = 56, 6 * 3 = 18, so 56 + 18 = 74.
User: What is 100 - 37 + 15?
Joe: 100 - 37 = 63, 63 + 15 = 78.
User: What is 5^3?
Joe: 5^3 = 5 * 5 * 5 = 25 * 5 = 125.
User: What is 2^8?
Joe: 2^8 = 256. We know 2^4 = 16, 2^8 = 16 * 16 = 256.
User: What is the area of a rectangle with length 12 and width 5?
Joe: Area = length * width = 12 * 5 = 60.
User: What is the perimeter of a square with side 9?
Joe: Perimeter = 4 * side = 4 * 9 = 36.
User: A circle has radius 7. What is its area? Use pi = 22/7.
Joe: Area = pi * r^2 = (22/7) * 7^2 = (22/7) * 49 = 22 * 7 = 154.
User: What is the hypotenuse of a right triangle with legs 3 and 4?
Joe: By Pythagoras: c^2 = 3^2 + 4^2 = 9 + 16 = 25, so c = sqrt(25) = 5.
User: What is the hypotenuse of a right triangle with legs 5 and 12?
Joe: c^2 = 5^2 + 12^2 = 25 + 144 = 169, so c = sqrt(169) = 13.
User: A triangle has base 10 and height 6. What is its area?
Joe: Area = (1/2) * base * height = (1/2) * 10 * 6 = 5 * 6 = 30.
User: You travel 60 mph for 2.5 hours. How far do you go?
Joe: Distance = speed * time = 60 * 2.5 = 150 miles.
User: You drive 180 miles at 60 mph. How long does it take?
Joe: Time = distance / speed = 180 / 60 = 3 hours.
User: An object falls from rest. Gravity is 10 m/s^2. How far in 3 seconds?
Joe: Distance = (1/2) * g * t^2 = (1/2) * 10 * 9 = 45 meters.
User: A car accelerates from 0 to 60 m/s in 10 seconds. What is its acceleration?
Joe: acceleration = (final - initial) / time = (60 - 0) / 10 = 6 m/s^2.
User: If you double a number and add 9, you get 25. What is the number?
Joe: 2x + 9 = 25, so 2x = 16, so x = 8.
User: Three consecutive integers sum to 72. What are they?
Joe: Let them be n, n+1, n+2. Then 3n + 3 = 72, so 3n = 69, n = 23. They are 23, 24, 25.
User: A jar has 3 red and 5 blue marbles. What is the probability of red?
Joe: Total = 3 + 5 = 8. Probability = 3/8.
User: Flip a coin twice. What is the probability of two heads?
Joe: Each flip is 1/2 for heads. So (1/2) * (1/2) = 1/4.
User: What is 0.75 as a fraction?
Joe: 0.75 = 75/100 = 3/4.
User: What is 3/5 as a decimal?
Joe: 3/5 = 3 / 5 = 0.6.
User: What is 15% as a fraction?
Joe: 15% = 15/100 = 3/20.
User: A number increased by 40% becomes 84. What was the original number?
Joe: 1.4x = 84, so x = 84 / 1.4 = 60.
User: A $500 item is discounted 30%, then an extra 10% off. What is the final price?
Joe: After 30% off: 500 * 0.70 = 350. Then 10% off: 350 * 0.90 = 315. Final price is $315.
User: What is the GCD of 24 and 36?
Joe: Factors of 24: 1,2,3,4,6,8,12,24. Factors of 36: 1,2,3,4,6,9,12,18,36. GCD = 12.
User: What is the LCM of 4 and 6?
Joe: Multiples of 4: 4,8,12,16. Multiples of 6: 6,12. LCM = 12.
User: What is 7! ?
Joe: 7! = 7 * 6 * 5 * 4 * 3 * 2 * 1 = 5040.
User: How many ways can you choose 2 items from 5?
Joe: C(5,2) = 5! / (2! * 3!) = (5 * 4) / 2 = 10.
User: What is log_2(64)?
Joe: 2^6 = 64, so log_2(64) = 6.
User: What is sqrt(196)?
Joe: 14^2 = 196, so sqrt(196) = 14.
User: What is the average of 10, 20, 30, 40, 50?
Joe: Sum = 10 + 20 + 30 + 40 + 50 = 150. Average = 150 / 5 = 30.
User: The mean of five numbers is 18. Four of them are 12, 15, 20, 25. What is the fifth?
Joe: Sum of four = 12 + 15 + 20 + 25 = 72. Total needed = 5 * 18 = 90. Fifth = 90 - 72 = 18.
User: A store sells apples for $2 each. How much for 7 apples?
Joe: 7 * 2 = 14. It costs $14.
User: If 5 apples cost $3, how much do 8 apples cost?
Joe: Each apple = 3/5 = 0.60 dollars. 8 apples = 8 * 0.60 = $4.80.
User: A car uses 8 liters per 100 km. How much for 350 km?
Joe: 350 / 100 = 3.5, then 3.5 * 8 = 28 liters.
User: You earn $15/hr for 32 hours. Tax is 10%. What do you take home?
Joe: Gross = 15 * 32 = 480. Tax = 480 * 0.10 = 48. Take home = 480 - 48 = $432.
User: If a rope is 3/4 meter and you cut off 1/3 meter, how much is left?
Joe: 3/4 - 1/3 = 9/12 - 4/12 = 5/12 meter.
User: A recipe needs 2.5 cups of flour for 10 cookies. How much for 40 cookies?
Joe: 40 / 10 = 4, so 4 * 2.5 = 10 cups.
User: What is 99 * 101?
Joe: 99 * 101 = (100 - 1)(100 + 1) = 100^2 - 1 = 10000 - 1 = 9999.
User: What is 23^2?
Joe: 23^2 = (20 + 3)^2 = 400 + 120 + 9 = 529.
User: What is 17 * 13?
Joe: 17 * 13 = (15 + 2)(15 - 2) = 15^2 - 4 = 225 - 4 = 221.
User: If a^2 = 49 and a > 0, what is a?
Joe: a^2 = 49, a > 0, so a = 7.
User: Solve: 5(x - 2) = 20.
Joe: 5(x - 2) = 20, so x - 2 = 4, so x = 6.
User: Solve: 3(2x + 1) = 27.
Joe: 3(2x + 1) = 27, so 2x + 1 = 9, so 2x = 8, so x = 4.
User: A triangle has angles 50 and 70 degrees. What is the third angle?
Joe: Sum of angles = 180, so third = 180 - 50 - 70 = 60 degrees.
User: Two angles are complementary and one is 35 degrees. What is the other?
Joe: Complementary means they sum to 90, so other = 90 - 35 = 55 degrees.
User: Two angles are supplementary and one is 110 degrees. What is the other?
Joe: Supplementary means they sum to 180, so other = 180 - 110 = 70 degrees.
User: A $2000 investment earns 5% simple interest per year. What is the interest after 3 years?
Joe: Interest = principal * rate * time = 2000 * 0.05 * 3 = $300.
User: A $1000 investment earns 8% compounded annually. What is the value after 2 years?
Joe: After year 1: 1000 * 1.08 = 1080. After year 2: 1080 * 1.08 = 1166.40. Value = $1166.40.
User: What is 1/2 + 1/3 + 1/6?
Joe: LCD = 6. So 3/6 + 2/6 + 1/6 = 6/6 = 1.
User: If 3/8 of a number is 15, what is the number?
Joe: (3/8)x = 15, so x = 15 * 8/3 = 40.
User: A speed is 90 km/h. What is it in m/s?
Joe: 90 km/h = 90 * 1000 / 3600 = 90000/3600 = 25 m/s.
User: A speed is 15 m/s. What is it in km/h?
Joe: 15 m/s = 15 * 3600 / 1000 = 54000/1000 = 54 km/h.
User: What is 36% of 250?
Joe: 36% of 250 = 250 * 0.36 = 90.
User: 45 is what percent of 180?
Joe: 45 / 180 = 0.25 = 25%.
User: A number is tripled then decreased by 7 to give 20. What is the number?
Joe: 3x - 7 = 20, so 3x = 27, so x = 9.
User: If you reverse the digits of a two-digit number, the result is 27 less. What is the number if the digits sum to 11?
Joe: Let tens digit be a, units be b. Then 10a + b - (10b + a) = 27, so 9a - 9b = 27, a - b = 3. Also a + b = 11. Adding: 2a = 14, a = 7, b = 4. Number is 74.
User: A rectangle has perimeter 36 and length 10. What is its width?
Joe: P = 2(l + w) = 36, so l + w = 18, so w = 18 - 10 = 8.
User: A rectangle has area 48 and width 6. What is its length?
Joe: Area = l * w = 48, so l = 48 / 6 = 8.
User: What is 11^3?
Joe: 11^3 = 11 * 11 * 11 = 121 * 11 = 1331.
User: What is the cube root of 216?
Joe: 6^3 = 216, so cube root of 216 = 6.
User: If you buy 4 notebooks at $3.50 each, how much change from $20?
Joe: 4 * 3.50 = 14.00. Change = 20 - 14 = $6.00.
User: A train travels 240 miles in 4 hours. What is its speed?
Joe: Speed = 240 / 4 = 60 mph.
User: Two trains start 300 miles apart and move toward each other at 50 and 70 mph. When do they meet?
Joe: Closing speed = 50 + 70 = 120 mph. Time = 300 / 120 = 2.5 hours.
User: A pool is being filled at 3 gallons per minute. It holds 360 gallons. How long to fill?
Joe: Time = 360 / 3 = 120 minutes = 2 hours.
User: What is 8 * 7 - 6 * 5 + 4 * 3?
Joe: 8 * 7 = 56, 6 * 5 = 30, 4 * 3 = 12. So 56 - 30 + 12 = 38.

Joe: 2(7) - 5 = 14 - 5 = 9.
User: Simplify: 3x + 5x - 2x when x = 4.
Joe: 3x + 5x - 2x = 6x. When x = 4, 6 * 4 = 24.
User: What is 1000 / 25?
Joe: 1000 / 25 = 40. Since 25 * 40 = 1000.
User: A pizza is cut into 8 slices. You eat 3. What fraction is left?
Joe: Left = 8 - 3 = 5 slices. Fraction left = 5/8.
User: If you can run a mile in 7 minutes, how long for 3 miles?
Joe: 3 * 7 = 21 minutes.
User: A ladder leans against a wall. Base is 6 feet from wall, top is 8 feet up. How long is the ladder?
Joe: By Pythagoras: L^2 = 6^2 + 8^2 = 36 + 64 = 100, so L = 10 feet.
User: What is the volume of a cube with side 5?
Joe: Volume = side^3 = 5^3 = 125.
User: A cylinder has radius 3 and height 10. What is its volume? Use pi = 3.14.
Joe: Volume = pi * r^2 * h = 3.14 * 9 * 10 = 3.14 * 90 = 282.6.
User: You deposit $500 at 6% interest compounded annually. What is it worth after 3 years?
Joe: After year 1: 500 * 1.06 = 530. After year 2: 530 * 1.06 = 561.80. After year 3: 561.80 * 1.06 = 595.508. Value = $595.51.
User: What is 4! / 3! ?
Joe: 4! = 24, 3! = 6, so 24 / 6 = 4. Or simply 4! / 3! = 4.
User: How many diagonals does a hexagon have?
Joe: Formula: n(n-3)/2 = 6(3)/2 = 9 diagonals.
User: A 12-foot rope is cut in a 3:1 ratio. How long is each piece?
Joe: Total parts = 3 + 1 = 4. Each part = 12 / 4 = 3 feet. Pieces are 9 feet and 3 feet.
User: If f(x) = 2x + 3, what is f(7)?
Joe: f(7) = 2(7) + 3 = 14 + 3 = 17.
User: If f(x) = x^2 - 1, what is f(4)?
Joe: f(4) = 4^2 - 1 = 16 - 1 = 15.
User: What is the slope of the line through (2, 3) and (6, 11)?
Joe: Slope = (11 - 3) / (6 - 2) = 8 / 4 = 2.
User: A line has slope 3 and passes through (1, 5). What is its y-intercept?
Joe: y = mx + b, so 5 = 3(1) + b, so b = 5 - 3 = 2. y-intercept is 2.
User: If you add 8 to a number and divide by 2, you get 15. What is the number?
Joe: (x + 8) / 2 = 15, so x + 8 = 30, so x = 22.
User: What is 2/3 of 45?
Joe: (2/3) * 45 = 90/3 = 30.
User: A batch of cookies makes 24. You need 156 cookies. How many batches?
Joe: 156 / 24 = 6.5, so you need 7 batches (rounding up since you can't make half a batch).
User: What is the median of 3, 7, 9, 12, 15?
Joe: Already sorted. The middle value is the 3rd: 9.
User: What is the mode of 4, 4, 7, 9, 4, 11?
Joe: 4 appears three times, more than any other number. Mode = 4.
User: A pair of pants costs $45 and is 20% off. What is the final price?
Joe: Discount = 45 * 0.20 = 9. Final price = 45 - 9 = $36.
User: How many seconds are in 3.5 minutes?
Joe: 3.5 * 60 = 210 seconds.

Joe: 1/3 = 2/6, so 5/6 - 2/6 = 3/6 = 1/2.

Joe: By Pythagoras: d^2 = 4^2 + 3^2 = 16 + 9 = 25, so d = 5 km.

Joe: After year 1: 20000 * 0.85 = 17000. After year 2: 17000 * 0.85 = 14450. Value = $14,450.
User: What is 0.125 as a fraction?
Joe: 0.125 = 125/1000 = 1/8.
User: If x^2 = 81, what are the possible values of x?
Joe: x^2 = 81, so x = 9 or x = -9.
User: A gym has 120 members. 45% are women. How many men?
Joe: Women = 120 * 0.45 = 54. Men = 120 - 54 = 66.
User: You mix 3 liters of 20% juice with 2 liters of 50% juice. What is the concentration?
Joe: Juice from first = 3 * 0.20 = 0.6. Juice from second = 2 * 0.50 = 1.0. Total juice = 1.6, total volume = 5. Concentration = 1.6/5 = 0.32 = 32%.
User: What is 729^(1/6)?
Joe: 3^6 = 729, so 729^(1/6) = 3.
User: A square has diagonal 10. What is its side?
Joe: For a square, diagonal = side * sqrt(2). So side = 10 / sqrt(2) = 10 * sqrt(2) / 2 = 5 * sqrt(2) ≈ 7.07.
User: You invest $1000 at 10% compounded annually. How much after 2 years?
Joe: Year 1: 1000 * 1.10 = 1100. Year 2: 1100 * 1.10 = 1210. Value = $1210.
User: What is 15 * 16?
Joe: 15 * 16 = 15 * 15 + 15 = 225 + 15 = 240.
User: A bag has 4 red, 3 blue, and 2 green marbles. What is the probability of NOT green?
Joe: Not green = 4 + 3 = 7 out of 9. Probability = 7/9.
User: What is the sum of all integers from 1 to 100?
Joe: Sum = n(n+1)/2 = 100 * 101 / 2 = 5050.
User: How many degrees in the interior angles of a pentagon?
Joe: Sum = (n-2) * 180 = 3 * 180 = 540 degrees.
User: What is 1/4 of 1/3?
Joe: (1/4) * (1/3) = 1/12.
User: A car travels the first half of a trip at 60 mph and the second half at 40 mph. What is the average speed for the whole trip?
Joe: Let total distance = 2d. Time for first half = d/60. Time for second half = d/40. Total time = d/60 + d/40 = 2d/120 + 3d/120 = 5d/120 = d/24. Average speed = 2d / (d/24) = 48 mph.
User: If the minute hand moves 6 degrees per minute, how many degrees in 25 minutes?
Joe: 25 * 6 = 150 degrees.
User: What is 999 + 1?
Joe: 999 + 1 = 1000.
User: Solve for x: x/5 = 12.
Joe: x/5 = 12, so x = 12 * 5 = 60.
User: A box is 12 inches long, 8 inches wide, and 10 inches tall. What is its volume?
Joe: Volume = 12 * 8 * 10 = 960 cubic inches.
User: A $30 item is marked up 50% then discounted 20%. What is the final price?
Joe: After markup: 30 * 1.50 = 45. After discount: 45 * 0.80 = 36. Final price = $36.
User: How many prime numbers are between 20 and 30?
Joe: Check: 21=3*7, 22=2*11, 23 is prime, 24=2*12, 25=5*5, 26=2*13, 27=3*9, 28=2*14, 29 is prime. Two primes: 23 and 29.
User: What is 6 * (8 - 3) + 2?
Joe: 6 * 5 + 2 = 30 + 2 = 32.
User: If you split $120 among 3 people in ratio 2:3:5, how much does each get?
Joe: Total parts = 2 + 3 + 5 = 10. Each part = 120 / 10 = 12. Shares: 24, 36, 60.

Joe: An increase of 25% multiplies the number by 1.25, so 1.25x equals 55. Dividing 55 by 1.25 gives 44.
User: A number decreased by 20% is 32. What was the number?
Joe: A 20% decrease leaves 80%, so 0.8x equals 32. Dividing 32 by 0.8 gives 40.
User: What is 30% of 150?
Joe: 10% of 150 is 15, so 30% is three times that, which is 45.
User: What is 15% of 80?
Joe: 10% of 80 is 8 and 5% of 80 is 4. Adding them gives 12, so 15% of 80 is 12.
User: What is 120% of 60?
Joe: 120% means the whole number plus 20% extra. 60 plus 20% of 60, which is 12, gives 72.
User: What is 12.5% of 80?
Joe: 12.5% is one eighth, and an eighth of 80 is 10. So 12.5% of 80 is 10.
User: What is 50% of 40% of 200?
Joe: 40% of 200 is 80, and 50% of 80 is 40. The answer is 40.

Joe: 10% of 30 is 3, so 90% is 30 minus 3, which is 27.
User: 24 is what percent of 60?
Joe: Divide 24 by 60 to get 0.4, which is 40%. So 24 is 40% of 60.
User: A $200 jacket is 40% off. What is the sale price?
Joe: 40% of 200 is 80, so the discount is 80 dollars. Taking 80 from 200 gives a sale price of $120.

Joe: 8% of 25 is 2, since 25 times 0.08 is 2. Adding that to 25 gives a total of 27 dollars.

Joe: 15% of 40 is 6, so the tip is 6 dollars. Adding it to 40 gives a total of 46 dollars.

Joe: The increase is 10 dollars. Divide 10 by 50 to get 0.20, which is a 20% increase.

Joe: The drop is 20 dollars. Divide 20 by 80 to get 0.25, which is a 25% decrease.

Joe: 30% of 45 is 13.50, so the discount is 13 dollars and 50 cents. The final price is 45 minus 13.50, which is $31.50.

Joe: A 25% discount means you pay 75% of the price. 75% of 64 is 48, so the sale price is $48.
User: What is 20% of 20% of 100?
Joe: 20% of 100 is 20, then 20% of 20 is 4. The answer is 4.

Joe: Multiply 3 by 2 dollars to get 6 dollars. So the oranges cost $6.

Joe: Four bags cost 4 times 9, which is 36 dollars. Change is 50 minus 36, so you get $14 back.

User: Tom is 3 times as old as his son, and Tom is 30. How old is the son?
Joe: Divide 30 by 3 to get 10. The son is 10 years old.
User: A mother is 40 and her daughter is 12. In how many years will the mother be twice as old as the daughter?
Joe: Let x be the number of years. Then 40 + x = 2(12 + x). That gives 40 + x = 24 + 2x, so x = 16. In 16 years the mother will be 56 and the daughter 28, exactly twice.
User: Bob is 7 years older than Sam, and their ages add to 41. How old are they?
Joe: Let Sam be s, so Bob is s + 7. Their sum is s + (s + 7) = 41, so 2s = 34 and s = 17. Sam is 17 and Bob is 24.

Joe: Divide 36 by 4 to get 9. The son is 9 years old.

User: Three years ago Max was half his current age. How old is Max?
Joe: If Max is m now, then m - 3 = m/2. Doubling both sides gives 2m - 6 = m, so m = 6. Max is 6.

User: Lindsay's age in 5 years will be twice her age 4 years ago. How old is Lindsay?
Joe: Let L be Lindsay's age now. Then L + 5 = 2(L - 4). Expanding gives L + 5 = 2L - 8, so L = 13. Lindsay is 13.
User: A father is 24 years older than his son. In 6 years he will be twice the son's age. How old is the son now?
Joe: Let the son be s, so the father is s + 24. In 6 years: s + 30 = 2(s + 6), so s + 30 = 2s + 12, giving s = 18. The son is 18 and the father is 42.
User: A train goes 60 mph for 3 hours. How far does it travel?
Joe: Distance equals speed times time, so 60 times 3 is 180. The train travels 180 miles.
User: A bike rides at 12 mph for 45 minutes. How far?
Joe: 45 minutes is three quarters of an hour. Three quarters of 12 is 9, so the bike travels 9 miles.
User: A plane flies at 500 mph for 2.5 hours. How far?
Joe: 500 times 2.5 is 1250. The plane covers 1,250 miles.
User: A car averages 40 mph and drives 320 miles. How long does the trip take?
Joe: Time equals distance divided by speed, so 320 divided by 40 is 8. The trip takes 8 hours.
User: A runner covers 5 km in 25 minutes. What is the speed in km/h?
Joe: 25 minutes is 25/60 of an hour, which is 5/12 hour. Speed is 5 divided by 5/12, and 5 times 12/5 is 12. The runner's speed is 12 km/h.
User: Two cities are 300 miles apart. Trains leave at the same time toward each other at 60 and 90 mph. When do they meet?
Joe: The closing speed is 60 + 90, which is 150 mph. Time equals 300 divided by 150, so 2 hours. They meet in 2 hours.

User: A drawer has 4 red and 4 blue socks. How many must you pull to guarantee a matching pair?
Joe: In the worst case you pull one red and one blue first, so the third sock must match one of them. You need 3 socks.
User: A farmer counts 30 heads and 74 legs among chickens and cows. How many cows are there?
Joe: Let cows be c, so chickens are 30 - c. Legs: 4c + 2(30 - c) = 74, so 4c + 60 - 2c = 74, giving 2c = 14 and c = 7. There are 7 cows and 23 chickens.
User: A clock reads 3:15. What is the angle between the hands?
Joe: The minute hand is on the 3, at 90 degrees. The hour hand has moved a quarter of the way from 3 to 4, which is 7.5 degrees. The angle between them is 7.5 degrees.
User: A ladder's rungs are 30 cm apart, the bottom rung is 30 cm off the ground, and the top rung is 30 cm below the top. The ladder is 2.4 m tall. How many rungs?
Joe: From the bottom rung to the top rung is 2.4 m minus 0.6 m of margins, which is 1.8 m, or 180 cm. That span is six 30 cm gaps, so the seven rungs sit at 30, 60, 90, 120, 150, 180, and 210 cm.
User: Which numbers between 10 and 20 are even and divisible by 3?
Joe: The multiples of 3 in that range are 12, 15, and 18. Of these, 12 and 18 are even.
User: Flip four coins. What is the probability of exactly two heads?
Joe: There are 16 possible outcomes, and there are 6 ways to pick which two coins show heads. The probability is 6/16, which reduces to 3/8.
User: Roll two dice. What is the probability of rolling a total of 7?
Joe: Rolls that total 7 are (1,6), (2,5), (3,4), (4,3), (5,2), and (6,1), 6 of the 36 outcomes. The probability is 6/36, which is 1/6.
User: A bag has 6 red and 4 blue marbles. What is the probability of drawing red?
Joe: There are 10 marbles and 6 are red, so the probability is 6/10, which reduces to 3/5.
User: One card is drawn from a deck of 52. What is the probability it is a heart?
Joe: There are 13 hearts in a deck. The probability is 13/52, which reduces to 1/4.
User: Pick a number from 1 to 10. What is the probability it is even?
Joe: The evens are 2, 4, 6, 8, and 10, which is 5 of the 10 numbers. The probability is 5/10, which is 1/2.
User: You have 8 identical coins and one is heavier. With a balance scale, what is the smallest number of weighings to find it?
Joe: Split the coins into 3, 3, and 2. Weigh 3 against 3; if they balance, the heavy coin is in the 2 and one more weighing finds it. Either way, two weighings are enough.
User: A 32-foot rope is halved again and again. How many halvings until the pieces are 1 foot?
Joe: Each halving divides by 2. Since 2^5 = 32, it takes 5 halvings to reach 1-foot pieces.
User: A man looks at a photo and says "Brothers and sisters have I none, but that man's father is my father's son." Who is in the photo?
Joe: My father's son is me, so the man's father is me. That makes the person in the photo my son.
User: A farmer has ducks and pigs, 7 animals with 22 legs in all. How many pigs?
Joe: Let pigs be p, so ducks are 7 - p. Legs: 4p + 2(7 - p) = 22, so 4p + 14 - 2p = 22, giving 2p = 8 and p = 4. There are 4 pigs and 3 ducks.
User: Three numbers are each 2 more than the previous and sum to 30. What are they?
Joe: Let them be n, n+2, and n+4. Then 3n + 6 = 30, so 3n = 24 and n = 8. They are 8, 10, and 12.
User: A 10-liter bucket is full. You pour out half, then half of what's left. How much remains?
Joe: After the first pour, 5 liters remain. Half of that is 2.5 liters, so 2.5 liters remain.

Joe: Each machine builds one widget in 3 minutes, so 100 machines finish 100 widgets in the same 3 minutes.
User: A shirt is $10. It goes 50% off, then another 50% off the sale price. What is the final price?
Joe: After the first 50% off it is 5 dollars. After the second 50% off it drops to 2.50. The final price is $2.50.
User: Fold a paper in half, then in half again. How many sections does it have?
Joe: Each fold doubles the layers. After two folds there are 2 times 2, which is 4 sections.
User: Which weighs more, a pound of feathers or a pound of bricks?
Joe: They weigh the same, because each is exactly one pound. The difference is only volume, not weight.

Joe: With the same numerator, a bigger denominator means a smaller value. So the order is 1/4, then 1/3, then 1/2.

Joe: 3/5 is 21/35 and 4/7 is 20/35. Since 21/35 is larger, 3/5 is bigger.

Joe: As eighths, these are 4/8, 5/8, and 6/8. The order is 1/2, 5/8, 3/4.
User: Which fraction is bigger, 2/3 or 3/5?
Joe: 2/3 is 10/15 and 3/5 is 9/15. Since 10/15 is larger, 2/3 is bigger.
User: Which is bigger, 3/7 or 2/5?
Joe: 3/7 is 15/35 and 2/5 is 14/35. Since 15/35 is larger, 3/7 is bigger.
User: Order 15%, 1/5, and 0.3 from smallest to largest.
Joe: 15% is 0.15, 1/5 is 0.20, and 0.3 is 0.30. The order is 15%, 1/5, 0.3.
User: What is the average of 4, 8, 12, and 16?
Joe: The sum is 4 + 8 + 12 + 16, which is 40, and there are 4 numbers. Dividing 40 by 4 gives an average of 10.
User: The average of three numbers is 7. Two of them are 5 and 9. What is the third?
Joe: The total must be 3 times 7, which is 21. The two known numbers sum to 14, so the third is 21 minus 14, which is 7.
User: What is the average of 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, and 6?
Joe: The sum is 21, and there are 6 numbers. Dividing 21 by 6 gives 3.5.
User: Six numbers average 15. You add a 7th number, 22. What is the new average?
Joe: The total before is 6 times 15, which is 90. Adding 22 gives 112, and dividing by the 7 numbers gives an average of 16.
User: Exam scores are 70, 80, 90, 85, and 75. What is the average?
Joe: The sum is 70 + 80 + 90 + 85 + 75, which is 400. Dividing by 5 gives an average of 80.
User: You need an average of 80 over 4 tests and have scored 70, 80, and 90. What must the 4th test be?
Joe: Four tests must total 4 times 80, which is 320. So far you have 70 + 80 + 90, which is 240. The fourth test must be 320 minus 240, which is 80.
User: What is the average of 8, 9, 10, 11, and 12?
Joe: The sum is 50, and there are 5 numbers. Dividing 50 by 5 gives 10.
User: What is the average of the first five counting numbers?
Joe: Those are 1, 2, 3, 4, and 5, which sum to 15. Dividing by 5 gives an average of 3.
User: A player scores 12, 8, 16, 20, and 14 in five games. What is the average?
Joe: The sum is 12 + 8 + 16 + 20 + 14, which is 70. Dividing by 5 gives an average of 14.
User: What is the remainder when 45 is divided by 7?
Joe: 7 times 6 is 42, leaving 3. So the remainder is 3.

Joe: 5 times 7 is 35, leaving 3. So the remainder is 3.

Joe: 9 times 11 is 99, leaving 1. So the remainder is 1.

Joe: 6 times 8 is 48, leaving 5. So the remainder is 5.

Joe: 8 times 8 is 64, leaving 7. So the remainder is 7.

Joe: 4 times 6 is 24, leaving 3. So the remainder is 3.

Joe: 9 times 9 is 81, leaving 1. So the remainder is 1.
User: Which numbers below 20 leave a remainder of 1 when divided by 5?
Joe: They are 1, 6, 11, and 16, since each is one more than a multiple of 5.
User: A number between 20 and 30 leaves a remainder of 4 when divided by 7. What is it?
Joe: The multiples of 7 nearby are 21 and 28. 21 plus 4 is 25, which fits, so the number is 25.

Joe: 6 times 7 is 42, so there are 7 full groups with 1 left over.
User: What is the remainder when 50 is divided by 8?
Joe: 8 times 6 is 48, leaving 2. The remainder is 2.
User: Estimate 19 times 8.
Joe: Round 19 to 20, then 20 times 8 is 160. The estimate is about 160.
User: Estimate 78 divided by 4.
Joe: Round 78 to 80, then 80 divided by 4 is 20. So the estimate is about 20.
User: Estimate 102 times 3.
Joe: Roughly speaking, 100 times 3 is 300. So the estimate is about 300.
User: Estimate 29 plus 53.
Joe: Round 29 up to 30 and 53 down to 50, so the estimate is 30 plus 50, about 80. The real answer, 82, is close to that.
User: Estimate 6.2 times 9.8.
Joe: Round 6.2 to 6 and 9.8 to 10, so 6 times 10 is 60. The estimate is about 60.
User: Round 376 to the nearest ten and to the nearest hundred.
Joe: For the nearest ten, 376 is between 370 and 380 and closer to 380. For the nearest hundred, it is closer to 400 than to 300. So 380 and 400.
User: Estimate 498 plus 247.
Joe: Round to 500 and 250, so the sum is about 750. The real answer is close to 750.
User: Estimate 2.95 times 4.

User: Roughly what is the square root of 48?
Joe: It is between 6 and 7 because 6^2 = 36 and 7^2 = 49. It sits very close to 7, about 6.9.
User: About how much is an 11% tip on a $61.20 dinner?
Joe: 10% is roughly 6 dollars and 1% is roughly 0.60 dollars. Adding them gives about 6.70, so the tip is close to $6.70.
User: Explain step by step: what is 5 + 4 * 3?
Joe: First handle the multiplication: 4 times 3 is 12. Then add 5, giving 17. So the answer is 17.
User: Walk me through finding 8 factorial.
Joe: 8! means 8 * 7 * 6 * 5 * 4 * 3 * 2 * 1. Multiplying in stages: 8 times 7 is 56, times 6 is 336, times 5 is 1680, times 4 is 6720, times 3 is 20160, times 2 is 40320, times 1 stays 40320. So 8! equals 40320.
User: Step by step, convert 2 hours to seconds.
Joe: Each hour is 60 minutes, so 2 hours is 120 minutes. Each minute is 60 seconds, so 120 times 60 is 7200 seconds.
User: Think step by step: how many minutes are in a day?
Joe: Each hour has 60 minutes and a day has 24 hours. So 24 times 60 is 1440 minutes.
User: Walk me through finding the volume of a pool that is 10 m long, 5 m wide, and 2 m deep.
Joe: Volume equals length times width times depth. So 10 times 5 is 50, then 50 times 2 is 100. The pool holds 100 cubic meters.
User: Explain step by step how to solve x/2 = 6.
Joe: The equation says x divided by 2 equals 6, so multiply both sides by 2. That gives x = 12.
User: Think step by step: what does a $15 book cost after 30% off?
Joe: 30% of 15 is 4.50, so the discount is 4.50. Subtracting from 15 gives 10.50, so the book costs $10.50.
User: Step by step, what is the median of 5, 1, 9, 3, and 7?
Joe: First sort them: 1, 3, 5, 7, 9. With five numbers the middle one is the third, which is 5. The median is 5.

User: How do I approach a problem I have never seen before?
Joe: Start by breaking the problem into smaller pieces. Write down what you know, what you need to find, and what constraints exist. Look for patterns or similar problems you have solved. Try a simple example by hand to see how it works. Build from there.

User: How do I think more logically?
Joe: Practice breaking decisions into smaller if-then steps. Write out your reasoning instead of jumping to conclusions. Question your assumptions. Look for evidence before forming opinions. The more you practice structured thinking, the more natural it becomes.

User: What is first principles thinking?
Joe: First principles thinking is breaking a problem down to its most basic truths and reasoning up from there, instead of reasoning by analogy or following what others do. It means questioning every assumption and building understanding from the ground up.

User: How do I make better decisions?
Joe: Gather the relevant information, identify your options, consider the consequences of each, check your biases, and then decide. Do not wait for perfect information. Most decisions are reversible, and indecision is often worse than a suboptimal choice.

User: What is the Socratic method?
Joe: The Socratic method is learning through asking questions rather than being told answers. By asking why, how, and what if repeatedly, you expose assumptions, test reasoning, and arrive at deeper understanding. It is one of the oldest and most effective teaching methods.

User: How do I solve a problem step by step?
Joe: First, understand the problem clearly. What exactly is being asked? Second, identify what information you have and what you need. Third, choose a strategy or approach. Fourth, execute step by step, checking your work. Fifth, verify your answer makes sense.

User: What does it mean to think critically?
Joe: Critical thinking means analyzing information objectively rather than accepting it at face value. It involves questioning sources, evaluating evidence, identifying biases, distinguishing facts from opinions, and drawing well-reasoned conclusions based on logic.

User: How do I learn from mistakes?
Joe: Acknowledge the mistake honestly. Ask what went wrong and why. Extract the lesson. Apply it next time. Do not repeat the mistake, and do not waste energy on guilt. Mistakes are data, not identity.

User: What is Occam's Razor?
Joe: Occam's Razor is the principle that among competing explanations, the simplest one that accounts for all the facts is usually correct. It does not guarantee the simplest answer is right, but it suggests starting there before adding unnecessary complexity.

User: What is metacognition?
Joe: Metacognition is thinking about your own thinking. It means being aware of how you learn, recognizing when you do not understand something, and adjusting your approach. Good learners constantly monitor their comprehension and adapt their strategies.

User: How do I avoid overthinking?
Joe: Set a deadline for your decision. Gather enough information to be informed but not so much that you drown in it. Ask yourself what the worst realistic outcome is. Most overthinking is fear of making the wrong choice disguised as thoroughness.

User: What is the difference between being smart and being wise?
Joe: Smart is the ability to solve problems quickly. Wisdom is knowing which problems are worth solving and how to apply knowledge with good judgment. A smart person knows a tomato is a fruit. A wise person does not put it in a fruit salad.

User: What is the Feynman Technique?
Joe: The Feynman Technique is a learning method: (1) Choose a concept, (2) Explain it in simple language as if teaching a child, (3) Identify gaps in your explanation, (4) Go back and fill the gaps, (5) Simplify and use analogies. If you cannot explain it simply, you do not understand it well enough.

User: What is 12 + 8 * 3 - 4?
Joe: Following order of operations, first do 8 times 3 which is 24, then 12 + 24 - 4 = 32.

User: What is 48 / (2 + 6)?
Joe: First the parentheses: 2 + 6 = 8, then 48 / 8 = 6.

User: What is 5 * (9 - 3) + 7?
Joe: Parentheses first: 9 - 3 = 6, then 5 * 6 = 30, then 30 + 7 = 37.

User: What is 100 - 3 * (4 + 2)?
Joe: Parentheses: 4 + 2 = 6, then multiply: 3 * 6 = 18, then subtract: 100 - 18 = 82.

User: What is (15 + 5) / (8 - 4)?
Joe: 15 + 5 = 20 and 8 - 4 = 4, so 20 / 4 = 5.

User: What is 2^3 * 4 + 6 / 3?
Joe: Exponent first: 2^3 = 8. Then 8 * 4 = 32 and 6 / 3 = 2. Finally 32 + 2 = 34.

User: What is 6 * 7 - 2 * (3 + 1)?
Joe: Parentheses: 3 + 1 = 4. Then 6 * 7 = 42 and 2 * 4 = 8. Finally 42 - 8 = 34.

User: What is (100 - 25) / 5 + 3 * 2?
Joe: 100 - 25 = 75, and 75 / 5 = 15. Also 3 * 2 = 6. So 15 + 6 = 21.

User: What is 144 / (12 / 3)?
Joe: Inner parentheses: 12 / 3 = 4, then 144 / 4 = 36.

User: What is 3^2 + 4^2 - 2^3?
Joe: 3^2 = 9, 4^2 = 16, and 2^3 = 8. Then 9 + 16 - 8 = 17.

User: What is 50 - 4 * (6 - 2) + 3?
Joe: Parentheses: 6 - 2 = 4. Then 4 * 4 = 16. So 50 - 16 + 3 = 37.

User: What is 200 / (10 * 2) + 5^2?
Joe: Parentheses: 10 * 2 = 20, so 200 / 20 = 10. Also 5^2 = 25. Then 10 + 25 = 35.

User: What is 11 * (2 + 7) - 9 * 3?
Joe: 2 + 7 = 9, so 11 * 9 = 99. And 9 * 3 = 27. Then 99 - 27 = 72.

User: What is (50 + 50) / (10 - 5)?
Joe: 50 + 50 = 100 and 10 - 5 = 5. Then 100 / 5 = 20.

User: A jacket costs $80 and is marked up 25%, then discounted 20%. What is the final price?
Joe: After markup: 80 * 1.25 = 100. After discount: 100 * 0.80 = 80. The final price is $80, the same as the original.

User: 40% of a number is 48. What is the number?
Joe: 0.4x = 48, so x = 48 / 0.4 = 120.

User: A price goes up 10% then up another 20%. What is the total percent increase?
Joe: After 10% up, the price is 1.10x. Then 20% more makes it 1.10 * 1.20x = 1.32x, a total increase of 32%.

User: You buy a $200 TV with 15% off, then pay 8% sales tax on the discounted price. What do you pay?
Joe: After 15% off: 200 * 0.85 = 170. Tax: 170 * 0.08 = 13.60. Total: 170 + 13.60 = $183.60.

User: A $1200 computer is on sale for 30% off, then an extra 10% coupon. What is the final price?
Joe: After 30% off: 1200 * 0.70 = 840. After 10% coupon: 840 * 0.90 = 756. Final price is $756.

User: What is 25% of 60% of 200?
Joe: 60% of 200 is 120, then 25% of 120 is 30. The answer is 30.

User: If your salary increases 10% two years in a row, what is the total percent increase from the start?
Joe: After two increases the salary is 1.10 * 1.10 = 1.21 times the original, a total increase of 21%.

User: A store buys a shirt for $12 and sells it for $20. What is the profit margin as a percentage of the selling price?
Joe: Profit is 20 - 12 = 8. As a percentage of selling price: 8 / 20 = 0.40 = 40%.

User: A car was bought for $15,000 and is now worth $10,500. What is the percent depreciation?
Joe: The drop is 15000 - 10500 = 4500. Percent decrease: 4500 / 15000 = 0.30 = 30%.

User: A population of 500 grows 8% one year then shrinks 5% the next. What is the final population?
Joe: After 8% growth: 500 * 1.08 = 540. After 5% decline: 540 * 0.95 = 513. Final population is 513.

User: You earn $3000 a month and spend 35% on rent, 25% on food, and 15% on transport. What percentage is left?
Joe: Total spent: 35% + 25% + 15% = 75%. That leaves 100% - 75% = 25% remaining.

Joe: 120 * 18 = 2160. You save $2160 in total.

User: A meal costs $45 and you want to leave a 18% tip on the pre-tax total. Tax is 8%. What do you pay including tax and tip?
Joe: Tax: 45 * 0.08 = 3.60. Tip: 45 * 0.18 = 8.10. Total: 45 + 3.60 + 8.10 = $56.70.

User: A recipe for 6 cookies uses 3/4 cup of sugar. How much sugar for 10 cookies?
Joe: 10 / 6 = 5/3 times the recipe. Sugar needed: (3/4) * (5/3) = 5/4 = 1 1/4 cups.

User: You have 5/8 of a pizza and eat 1/3 of what you have. How much is left?
Joe: You eat (1/3) * (5/8) = 5/24. Left: 5/8 - 5/24 = 15/24 - 5/24 = 10/24 = 5/12.

User: Which is smaller, 5/9 or 7/12?
Joe: 5/9 = 20/36 and 7/12 = 21/36. Since 20/36 is smaller, 5/9 is smaller.

Joe: Total parts = 3 + 7 = 10. Each part = 1000 / 10 = 100. The shares are $300 and $700.

Joe: Total parts = 2 + 5 = 7. Each part = 42 / 7 = 6. The numbers are 12 and 30.

User: Two cars drive toward each other from 540 miles apart, one at 75 mph and the other at 60 mph. When do they meet?
Joe: Closing speed = 75 + 60 = 135 mph. Time = 540 / 135 = 4 hours.

User: Two pipes fill a tank. One does it in 6 hours, the other in 10 hours. How long together?
Joe: Rates: 1/6 and 1/10 per hour. Combined: 5/30 + 3/30 = 8/30 = 4/15 per hour. Time = 15/4 = 3.75 hours.

User: A can fill a pool in 5 hours, B can fill it in 3 hours. If both run, how long to fill?
Joe: Rates: 1/5 and 1/3 per hour. Combined: 3/15 + 5/15 = 8/15 per hour. Time = 15/8 = 1.875 hours.

User: Three workers together take 4 hours to paint a house. If one alone takes 12 hours, what is the combined rate of the other two?
Joe: Total rate is 1/4 per hour. One worker's rate is 1/12. The other two together: 1/4 - 1/12 = 3/12 - 1/12 = 2/12 = 1/6 per hour.

User: Alice can do a job in 8 hours. Bob can do it in 12 hours. How long if Alice works 2 hours alone, then Bob joins?
Joe: In 2 hours Alice does 2/8 = 1/4 of the job. Remaining: 3/4. Together rate: 1/8 + 1/12 = 5/24 per hour. Time: (3/4) / (5/24) = 18/5 = 3.6 hours.

User: A machine makes 200 items in 8 hours. How long for 3 machines to make 1000 items?
Joe: One machine rate: 200/8 = 25 items per hour. Three machines: 75 items per hour. Time: 1000 / 75 = 13.33 hours.

User: Pipe A fills a tank in 4 hours, pipe B fills it in 6 hours, and pipe C drains it in 12 hours. All three open, how long to fill?
Joe: Fill rates: 1/4 + 1/6 = 5/12 per hour. Drain rate: 1/12 per hour. Net: 5/12 - 1/12 = 4/12 = 1/3 per hour. Time = 3 hours.

User: A job pays $480 and two workers split it based on hours worked. Worker A does 5 hours and Worker B does 3 hours. How much does each get?
Joe: Total hours = 8. A gets 5/8 of 480 = $300. B gets 3/8 of 480 = $180.

User: Three pipes together fill a pool in 5 hours. If pipe A alone takes 10 hours and B alone takes 15 hours, how long for C alone?
Joe: Combined rate is 1/5. A's rate is 1/10, B's is 1/15. C's rate: 1/5 - 1/10 - 1/15 = 6/30 - 3/30 - 2/30 = 1/30. C alone takes 30 hours.

User: Anna is 3 times as old as Ben. In 10 years she will be twice his age. How old is Ben now?
Joe: Let Ben = b, so Anna = 3b. In 10 years: 3b + 10 = 2(b + 10), so 3b + 10 = 2b + 20, giving b = 10. Ben is 10.

User: A mother is 36 and her son is 8. In how many years will she be exactly twice his age?
Joe: Let x be years. Then 36 + x = 2(8 + x), so 36 + x = 16 + 2x, giving x = 20. In 20 years she will be 56 and he will be 28.

User: Tom is 5 years older than Sam. In 7 years the sum of their ages will be 49. How old is Sam now?
Joe: Let Sam = s, Tom = s + 5. In 7 years: (s + 7) + (s + 5 + 7) = 49, so 2s + 19 = 49, giving s = 15. Sam is 15.

User: Mary is twice as old as her daughter. Four years ago she was three times as old. How old is the daughter now?
Joe: Let daughter = d, Mary = 2d. Four years ago: 2d - 4 = 3(d - 4), so 2d - 4 = 3d - 12, giving d = 8. The daughter is 8.

User: A father is 30 years older than his son. In 4 years he will be twice the son's age. How old is the son now?
Joe: Let son = s, father = s + 30. In 4 years: s + 34 = 2(s + 4), so s + 34 = 2s + 8, giving s = 26. The son is 26.

User: In how many years will a father who is 40 be exactly 3 times as old as his daughter who is 10?
Joe: 40 + x = 3(10 + x), so 40 + x = 30 + 3x, giving 2x = 10 and x = 5. In 5 years he will be 45 and she will be 15.

User: Grandma is 72, which is 8 times her grandchild's age. How old will the grandchild be when Grandma is 80?
Joe: Grandchild is 72 / 8 = 9 now. When Grandma is 80 that will be 8 years later, so the grandchild will be 17.

User: Rachel is 4 times as old as her son. Five years ago she was 7 times his age. How old is her son now?
Joe: Let son = s, Rachel = 4s. Five years ago: 4s - 5 = 7(s - 5), so 4s - 5 = 7s - 35, giving 3s = 30 and s = 10. The son is 10.

User: A brother is 6 years older than his sister. In 3 years their ages will add to 39. How old is the sister now?
Joe: Let sister = s, brother = s + 6. In 3 years: (s + 3) + (s + 9) = 39, so 2s + 12 = 39, giving s = 13.5. The sister is 13.5.

User: Dad is 50 and his daughter is 14. How many years ago was he exactly 4 times her age?
Joe: Let x be years ago. 50 - x = 4(14 - x), so 50 - x = 56 - 4x, giving 3x = 6 and x = 2. Two years ago he was 48 and she was 12.

User: Two cars start from the same point. One drives north at 50 mph, the other east at 70 mph. How far apart after 2 hours?
Joe: North car goes 100 miles, east car goes 140 miles. By Pythagoras: d = sqrt(100^2 + 140^2) = sqrt(10000 + 19600) = sqrt(29600) = about 172 miles.

User: A boat goes 18 km upstream in 3 hours and 24 km downstream in 3 hours. What is the speed of the current?
Joe: Upstream speed = 18/3 = 6 km/h. Downstream speed = 24/3 = 8 km/h. Current = (8 - 6) / 2 = 1 km/h.

User: A plane flies 500 mph in still air. It flies 1200 miles with a 50 mph tailwind. How long does it take?
Joe: Effective speed = 500 + 50 = 550 mph. Time = 1200 / 550 = about 2.18 hours, roughly 2 hours 11 minutes.

User: Two trains leave the same station at the same time on parallel tracks, one at 80 mph and the other at 60 mph. How far apart after 3 hours?
Joe: They separate at 80 + 60 = 140 mph. After 3 hours: 140 * 3 = 420 miles apart.

User: You walk north 3 km then east 4 km then south 1 km. How far are you from the start?
Joe: You end up 2 km north (3 - 1 = 2) and 4 km east of the start. Distance = sqrt(2^2 + 4^2) = sqrt(4 + 16) = sqrt(20) = about 4.47 km.

User: A runner does a lap in 90 seconds. How many laps in 12 minutes?
Joe: 12 minutes = 720 seconds. Laps = 720 / 90 = 8 laps.

User: Two cars are 300 miles apart, both heading the same direction. The faster car behind goes 70 mph, the slower ahead goes 50 mph. How long to catch up?
Joe: Closing speed = 70 - 50 = 20 mph. Time = 300 / 20 = 15 hours.

User: A boat goes 20 km downstream in 2 hours and the same 20 km back upstream in 5 hours. What is the boat's speed in still water?
Joe: Downstream = 10 km/h, upstream = 4 km/h. Boat speed = (10 + 4) / 2 = 7 km/h.

Joe: Each apple costs 2/3 of a dollar. 7 apples = 7 * (2/3) = 14/3 = $4.67.

Joe: 900 / 150 = 6 months exactly.

User: You buy paint for $32, brushes for $8, and tape for $5. What is the total?
Joe: 32 + 8 + 5 = $45.

User: A bag has 5 red and 3 blue marbles. What is the probability of drawing 2 red in a row without replacement?
Joe: First draw: 5/8. Second draw: 4/7. Probability = (5/8) * (4/7) = 20/56 = 5/14.

User: Roll two dice. What is the probability that one die shows a number twice the other?
Joe: Favorable pairs: (1,2), (2,1), (2,4), (4,2), (3,6), (6,3) = 6 of 36 outcomes. Probability = 6/36 = 1/6.

User: Flip three coins. What is the probability of exactly one head?
Joe: The outcomes with exactly one head: H TT, T HT, T TH = 3 of 8. Probability = 3/8.

User: A bag has 4 red, 3 green, and 5 yellow marbles. What is the probability of drawing yellow?
Joe: Total = 12 marbles. Yellow = 5. Probability = 5/12.

User: What is the probability of rolling a sum of 9 with two dice?
Joe: Pairs that sum to 9: (3,6), (4,5), (5,4), (6,3) = 4 of 36. Probability = 4/36 = 1/9.

User: A deck has 52 cards. You draw 2 without replacement. What is the probability both are spades?
Joe: First spade: 13/52. Second: 12/51. Probability = (13/52) * (12/51) = 156/2652 = 1/17.

User: You roll a die twice. What is the probability both rolls are the same?
Joe: There are 6 matching pairs out of 36 outcomes. Probability = 6/36 = 1/6.

User: A bag has 6 white and 4 black marbles. Draw 2 with replacement. What is the probability of 2 white?
Joe: Each draw: 6/10. Both white: (6/10)^2 = 36/100 = 9/25.

User: You draw one card from a standard deck. What is the probability it is either a king or a heart?
Joe: P(king) = 4/52, P(heart) = 13/52, P(king of hearts) = 1/52. By inclusion-exclusion: 4/52 + 13/52 - 1/52 = 16/52 = 4/13.

User: Roll a die 4 times. What is the probability that at least one roll is a 6?
Joe: P(no 6 in 4 rolls) = (5/6)^4 = 625/1296. P(at least one 6) = 1 - 625/1296 = 671/1296.

User: A bag has 3 red and 7 blue marbles. Draw 3 without replacement. What is the probability all 3 are blue?
Joe: (7/10) * (6/9) * (5/8) = 210/720 = 7/24.

User: Flip a coin until you get heads. What is the probability you need exactly 3 flips?
Joe: You need tails, tails, then heads. Probability = (1/2)^3 = 1/8.

User: A bag has 2 red, 3 green, and 5 blue marbles. Draw 2 without replacement. What is P(both green)?
Joe: (3/10) * (2/9) = 6/90 = 1/15.

User: What is the area of a trapezoid with parallel sides 6 and 10 and height 5?
Joe: Area = (1/2)(6 + 10) * 5 = (1/2)(16)(5) = 40.

User: What is the surface area of a sphere with radius 3? Use pi = 3.14.
Joe: SA = 4 * pi * r^2 = 4 * 3.14 * 9 = 113.04.

User: What is the volume of a cone with radius 3 and height 7? Use pi = 3.14.
Joe: V = (1/3) * pi * r^2 * h = (1/3) * 3.14 * 9 * 7 = (1/3) * 197.82 = 65.94.

User: A rectangle is 10 cm by 6 cm. What is the length of its diagonal?
Joe: d^2 = 10^2 + 6^2 = 100 + 36 = 136, so d = sqrt(136) = about 11.66 cm.

User: A circular pool has a diameter of 10 feet. What is its circumference? Use pi = 3.14.
Joe: C = pi * d = 3.14 * 10 = 31.4 feet.

User: A box has length 8, width 5, and height 3. What is the length of the space diagonal?
Joe: d = sqrt(8^2 + 5^2 + 3^2) = sqrt(64 + 25 + 9) = sqrt(98) = about 9.90.

User: A cylinder has radius 4 and height 10. What is its volume? Use pi = 3.14.
Joe: V = pi * r^2 * h = 3.14 * 16 * 10 = 502.4.

User: What is the area of a triangle with vertices at (0,0), (6,0), and (3,4)?
Joe: Base = 6, height = 4. Area = (1/2) * 6 * 4 = 12.

User: A square has diagonal 8. What is its area?
Joe: Side = 8 / sqrt(2) = 4 * sqrt(2). Area = side^2 = (4 * sqrt(2))^2 = 16 * 2 = 32.

User: A circular garden has radius 5 m. A path 2 m wide surrounds it. What is the area of the path? Use pi = 3.14.
Joe: Outer area = pi * 7^2 = 3.14 * 49 = 153.86. Inner area = pi * 5^2 = 3.14 * 25 = 78.5. Path area = 153.86 - 78.5 = 75.36 m^2.

User: A sphere has volume 36pi cubic units. What is its radius?
Joe: V = (4/3)pi*r^3 = 36pi, so r^3 = 27 and r = 3.

User: What is the perimeter of a right triangle with legs 5 and 12?
Joe: Hypotenuse = sqrt(25 + 144) = sqrt(169) = 13. Perimeter = 5 + 12 + 13 = 30.

User: A cone has volume 100pi and height 10. What is its radius?
Joe: V = (1/3)pi*r^2*h = 100pi, so (1/3)*r^2*10 = 100, giving r^2 = 30 and r = sqrt(30) = about 5.48.

User: You have a 4 by 4 grid. How many squares of any size are in it?
Joe: 1x1: 16, 2x2: 9, 3x3: 4, 4x4: 1. Total = 16 + 9 + 4 + 1 = 30.

User: At 2:00, what is the angle between the hour and minute hands of a clock?
Joe: Minute hand at 12 (0 degrees), hour hand at 2 (60 degrees). Angle = 60 degrees.

User: How many minutes does the minute hand travel between 12:00 and 12:47?
Joe: The minute hand is at 12 at 0 minutes and at 47 minutes. That is 47 minutes = 282 degrees.

User: A clock shows 6:00. What is the smaller angle between the hands?
Joe: Minute hand at 12, hour hand at 6, pointing in opposite directions. Angle = 180 degrees.

User: What day is it 45 days after a Wednesday?
Joe: 45 / 7 = 6 weeks and 3 days. Three days after Wednesday is Saturday.

User: A flight leaves New York at 2:00 PM Eastern and lands in Los Angeles at 4:30 PM Pacific. What is the flight duration?
Joe: LA is 3 hours behind NY. 2:00 PM Eastern = 11:00 AM Pacific. Flight time = 4:30 - 11:00 AM = 5.5 hours.

User: You start walking at 3:15 PM and finish at 5:40 PM. How long did you walk?
Joe: 3:15 to 5:15 is 2 hours. 5:15 to 5:40 is 25 minutes. Total = 2 hours 25 minutes.

User: A movie starts at 7:45 PM and runs for 2 hours 10 minutes. What time does it end?
Joe: 7:45 + 2 hours = 9:45. Then + 10 minutes = 9:55 PM.

User: It is 3:00 PM in Chicago (Central). What time is it in New York (Eastern)?
Joe: Eastern is 1 hour ahead of Central. So it is 4:00 PM in New York.

User: Which is the better deal: 5 items for $8 or 3 items for $5?
Joe: Per item: 5 for $8 = $1.60 each, 3 for $5 = $1.67 each. Five for eight dollars is the better deal.

User: Sort these from smallest to largest: 3/8, 2/5, 1/3, 1/4.
Joe: As decimals: 0.25, 0.333, 0.375, 0.4. Order: 1/4, 1/3, 3/8, 2/5.

User: Which is larger, 2/5 or 3/8?
Joe: 2/5 = 16/40 and 3/8 = 15/40. Since 16/40 is larger, 2/5 is bigger.

User: A store sells cereal in two boxes: 12 oz for $3.50 and 20 oz for $5.20. Which is cheaper per ounce?
Joe: 12 oz: 3.50 / 12 = about 29 cents per oz. 20 oz: 5.20 / 20 = 26 cents per oz. The larger box is cheaper per ounce.

User: Compare: is 5/6 greater than 7/9?
Joe: 5/6 = 15/18 and 7/9 = 14/18. Yes, 15/18 is larger, so 5/6 is greater.

User: You can buy 6 notebooks for $9 or 4 for $7. Which deal costs less per notebook?
Joe: 6 for $9 = $1.50 each. 4 for $7 = $1.75 each. Six for nine dollars is cheaper.

User: A runner completes a 10K in 48 minutes. What is their average pace per mile?
Joe: 10K is about 6.2 miles. 48 / 6.2 = about 7.74 minutes per mile, roughly 7 minutes 44 seconds per mile.

User: A job is supposed to take 120 hours. Two workers do 30 hours each and a third does 45 hours. How many hours remain?
Joe: Hours done: 30 + 30 + 45 = 105. Remaining: 120 - 105 = 15 hours.

User: Three consecutive even numbers sum to 42. What are they?
Joe: Let them be n, n+2, n+4. Then 3n + 6 = 42, so 3n = 36 and n = 12. They are 12, 14, 16.

User: A number doubled then increased by 12 gives 50. What is the number?
Joe: 2x + 12 = 50, so 2x = 38 and x = 19.

User: If you subtract 9 from a number and then halve the result, you get 7. What is the number?
Joe: (x - 9) / 2 = 7, so x - 9 = 14, giving x = 23.

User: Four times a number minus twice the same number equals 18. What is the number?
Joe: 4x - 2x = 18, so 2x = 18 and x = 9.

User: A rectangle has area 72 and one side is 9. What is the other side?
Joe: 72 / 9 = 8. The other side is 8.

User: What is the volume of a rectangular box that is 7 by 4 by 3?
Joe: 7 * 4 * 3 = 84 cubic units.

User: A square garden has side 12 meters. What is its area?
Joe: 12^2 = 144 square meters.

User: A rectangle has length 15 and width 6. What is its area?
Joe: 15 * 6 = 90.

User: A box is 10 inches long, 8 inches wide, and 6 inches tall. What is its volume?
Joe: 10 * 8 * 6 = 480 cubic inches.

User: You mix 2 liters of juice with 3 liters of water. What fraction of the mixture is juice?
Joe: Juice = 2 liters out of 5 total. Fraction = 2/5.

User: A recipe calls for 2.5 cups of flour and you want to make 3 times the recipe. How much flour?
Joe: 2.5 * 3 = 7.5 cups.

User: A line passes through (1, 3) and (4, 12). What is its slope?
Joe: Slope = (12 - 3) / (4 - 1) = 9 / 3 = 3.

User: What is the equation of a line with slope 2 passing through (0, 5)?
Joe: y = 2x + 5.

User: What is the distance between (1, 2) and (4, 6)?
Joe: d = sqrt((4-1)^2 + (6-2)^2) = sqrt(9 + 16) = sqrt(25) = 5.

User: A coin is flipped 4 times. What is the probability of getting 3 heads and 1 tail?
Joe: C(4,3) = 4 ways to place 3 heads. Each sequence has probability 1/16. Probability = 4/16 = 1/4.

User: If you roll a die, what is the probability of getting a number less than 3 or greater than 4?
Joe: Numbers less than 3: 1, 2. Numbers greater than 4: 5, 6. That is 4 of 6 outcomes. Probability = 4/6 = 2/3.

User: A card is drawn from a deck. What is the probability it is a number card (2 through 10)?
Joe: Number cards: 9 per suit times 4 suits = 36. Probability = 36/52 = 9/13.

User: Two dice are rolled. What is the probability the sum is greater than 9?
Joe: Sums > 9: 10 (3 ways), 11 (2 ways), 12 (1 way) = 6 of 36. Probability = 6/36 = 1/6.

User: From a bag of 8 marbles (5 blue, 3 red), you draw 2 without replacement. What is the probability of exactly 1 red?
Joe: Blue then red: (5/8)(3/7) = 15/56. Red then blue: (3/8)(5/7) = 15/56. Total = 30/56 = 15/28.

User: A bag has 4 red and 6 green marbles. Draw 2 with replacement. What is P(one red and one green)?
Joe: Red then green: (4/10)(6/10) = 24/100. Green then red: (6/10)(4/10) = 24/100. Total = 48/100 = 12/25.

User: A jar has 10 coins: 7 dimes and 3 quarters. You pick 2 at random. What is the probability both are quarters?
Joe: (3/10)(2/9) = 6/90 = 1/15.

User: Two balls are drawn from a bag of 4 white, 3 black, without replacement. What is P(both white)?
Joe: (4/7)(3/6) = 12/42 = 2/7.

User: What is the sum of the first 50 positive integers?
Joe: Sum = 50 * 51 / 2 = 1275.

User: How many prime numbers are there between 50 and 70?
Joe: Primes: 53, 59, 61, 67. There are 4 primes.

User: What is 12! / 10! ?
Joe: 12! / 10! = 12 * 11 = 132.

User: What is sqrt(144) + sqrt(81)?
Joe: sqrt(144) = 12, sqrt(81) = 9. Then 12 + 9 = 21.

User: How many factors does 36 have?
Joe: Factors: 1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 9, 12, 18, 36. There are 9 factors.

User: What is the 8th term of the sequence 3, 6, 12, 24, ...?
Joe: Each term doubles. 3, 6, 12, 24, 48, 96, 192, 384. The 8th term is 384.

User: What is the sum of the first 6 even numbers?
Joe: 2 + 4 + 6 + 8 + 10 + 12 = 42.

User: How many ways can 5 people sit in a row?
Joe: 5! = 5 * 4 * 3 * 2 * 1 = 120.

User: A train travels 180 miles in 3 hours. What is its speed in km/h?
Joe: Speed = 180 / 3 = 60 mph. In km/h: 60 * 1.609 = about 96.5 km/h.

User: 3 workers together take 6 hours to complete a job. Worker A alone takes 15 hours. How long for Workers B and C working together?
Joe: Total rate = 1/6. A's rate = 1/15. B+C rate = 1/6 - 1/15 = 5/30 - 2/30 = 3/30 = 1/10. B and C together take 10 hours.

User: A father is 45 and his son is 15. How many years ago was the father 5 times as old as the son?
Joe: Let x years ago. 45 - x = 5(15 - x), so 45 - x = 75 - 5x, giving 4x = 30 and x = 7.5. So 7.5 years ago.

User: What is 0.2 as a fraction in simplest form?
Joe: 0.2 = 2/10 = 1/5.

User: A movie ticket costs $12. A bag of popcorn costs $8. A drink costs $5. How much for 2 of each?
Joe: One set costs 12 + 8 + 5 = 25. Two sets cost 25 * 2 = $50.

Joe: Total hours = 7.5 + 6.5 = 14. Pay = 14 * 18 = $252.

User: You buy 3 pounds of bananas at $1.50 per pound and 2 pounds of apples at $2.25 per pound. What is the total?

Joe: Monthly: 30 * 24 = 720. Total: 720 + 100 = $820.

Joe: Sum = (4 - 2) * 180 = 360 degrees.

Joe: n(n-3)/2 = 5(2)/2 = 5 diagonals.

Joe: V = pi*r^2*h = pi*4*h = 40pi, so h = 10.

Joe: Closing speed = 55 + 45 = 100 mph. Time = 400 / 100 = 4 hours.

Joe: 56 / 7 = 8. Width is 8.

User: What is the area of a regular hexagon with side length 4?

User: You walk 5 km in 1 hour, then 3 km in 40 minutes. What is your overall average speed in km/h?

User: What is 999 / 9?

Joe: C(7,3) = 7! / (3! * 4!) = (7 * 6 * 5) / (3 * 2 * 1) = 35.

User: A rectangle is twice as long as it is wide. The perimeter is 36. What are the dimensions?

User: What is 1/3 + 2/5?

User: A ladder is 13 feet long. It leans against a wall with its base 5 feet from the wall. How high does it reach?

User: A ball is dropped from a height of 45 meters. It bounces back to half the height each time. What is the total distance traveled when it hits the ground the third time?

User: A hexagon has interior angles that sum to how many degrees?
Joe: Sum = (6-2) * 180 = 4 * 180 = 720 degrees.

User: How many 3-letter arrangements can you make from the letters ABC without repeating?

User: A bus holds 48 passengers. If 3 buses make the trip and 15 seats are empty total, how many people rode?

Joe: Sum = 85 + 92 + 78 + 90 = 345. Average = 345 / 4 = 86.25.

User: If you divide a number by 4 and then multiply by 3, you get 18. What is the number?
Joe: (x / 4) * 3 = 18, so 3x/4 = 18, so x = 18 * 4/3 = 24.

User: A cylinder has radius 5 and height 8. Use pi = 3.14. What is the volume?
Joe: Volume = pi * r^2 * h = 3.14 * 25 * 8 = 3.14 * 200 = 628.

User: You need an average of 90 on five tests. Your scores are 85, 88, 92, 91. What must the fifth test be?
Joe: Total needed = 5 * 90 = 450. Current sum = 85 + 88 + 92 + 91 = 356. Fifth test = 450 - 356 = 94.

User: What is 3/8 as a decimal?
Joe: 3 / 8 = 0.375.

User: A triangle has base 14 and height 5. What is the area?
Joe: Area = (1/2) * 14 * 5 = 7 * 5 = 35.

User: What is 20 * (5 - 3) + 10?
Joe: 20 * 2 + 10 = 40 + 10 = 50.

User: A store marks up a $40 item by 35%. What is the selling price?
Joe: 35% of 40 = 14. Selling price = 40 + 14 = $54.

User: What is the probability of rolling a number greater than 4 on a standard die?
Joe: Numbers greater than 4 are 5 and 6, which is 2 out of 6. Probability = 2/6 = 1/3.

User: How many seconds are in 2.5 hours?
Joe: 2.5 * 3600 = 9,000 seconds.

User: A right triangle has legs 9 and 12. What is the hypotenuse?
Joe: c^2 = 9^2 + 12^2 = 81 + 144 = 225, so c = 15.

User: A jar has 30 marbles: 12 red, 8 blue, and 10 green. What is the probability of NOT drawing red?
Joe: Not red = 8 + 10 = 18. Probability = 18/30 = 3/5.

User: What is 7 squared minus 5 squared?
Joe: 7^2 = 49, 5^2 = 25. 49 - 25 = 24.

User: You travel 120 km in 1.5 hours, then 80 km in 1 hour. What is the average speed?
Joe: Total distance = 120 + 80 = 200 km. Total time = 1.5 + 1 = 2.5 hours. Average speed = 200 / 2.5 = 80 km/h.

User: A number is 8 more than twice another. The two numbers add to 26. What are they?
Joe: Let x be the smaller. Then the larger is 2x + 8. x + 2x + 8 = 26, so 3x = 18, x = 6. Numbers are 6 and 20.

User: What is 32 divided by 5 with remainder?
Joe: 5 * 6 = 30, remainder = 2.

User: You put $200 in a savings account earning 3% per year. How much after 2 years with simple interest?
Joe: Interest = 200 * 0.03 * 2 = 12. Total = 212.

User: Convert 3.5 km to meters.
Joe: 3.5 km * 1000 = 3,500 meters.

User: A field is 120 meters long and 80 meters wide. What is its area in square meters?
Joe: Area = 120 * 80 = 9,600 square meters.

User: Two numbers multiply to 48 and add to 19. What are they?
Joe: They are 3 and 16. Check: 3 * 16 = 48 and 3 + 16 = 19.

User: A factory produces 150 items per hour. 10% are defective. How many are good?
Joe: Defective = 150 * 0.10 = 15. Good = 150 - 15 = 135.

User: A parking lot has 3 levels with 45 spaces each. If 80 cars are parked, how many spaces are free?
Joe: Total spaces = 3 * 45 = 135. Free = 135 - 80 = 55.

User: What is the slope of a horizontal line?
Joe: A horizontal line has rise 0 and some run, so slope = 0 / run = 0.

User: You earn $500 per week and spend $325. What percent do you save?
Joe: Saved = 500 - 325 = 175. Percent saved = 175 / 500 = 0.35 = 35%.

User: How many minutes from 9:45 AM to 12:15 PM?
Joe: 9:45 to 11:45 is 120 minutes, then to 12:15 is 30 more. Total = 150 minutes.

User: What is 10% of 20% of 500?
Joe: 20% of 500 = 100. 10% of 100 = 10.

User: A box contains 20 books. You remove 5, then add 8. How many are in the box now?
Joe: 20 - 5 + 8 = 23 books.

User: 3 consecutive multiples of 4 sum to 84. What are they?
Joe: Let them be 4n, 4(n+1), 4(n+2). Then 12n + 12 = 84, so 12n = 72, n = 6. They are 24, 28, 32.

User: A triangle has angles in ratio 2:3:4. What are the angles?
Joe: Sum of parts = 2 + 3 + 4 = 9. Each part = 180 / 9 = 20. Angles are 40, 60, 80 degrees.

User: A store has 200 items on sale at 40% off and another 100 at 25% off. The average original price is $50. What is the total discount given?
Joe: First group discount: 200 * 50 * 0.40 = 4,000. Second group: 100 * 50 * 0.25 = 1,250. Total discount = $5,250.

User: The average of 5 numbers is 12. If you remove one that is 8, what is the new average?
Joe: Total = 5 * 12 = 60. After removing 8, sum = 52 over 4 numbers. New average = 52 / 4 = 13.

User: A string is cut in half, then each half is cut in half. What fraction of the original is the shortest piece?
Joe: First cut gives halves. Second cut gives quarters. The shortest piece is 1/4 of the original.

User: A farmer has 3 times as many cows as sheep. If he has 48 animals total, how many cows?
Joe: Let sheep = s, cows = 3s. s + 3s = 48, so 4s = 48, s = 12. Cows = 36.

User: What is 1000 - 467?
Joe: 1000 - 467 = 1000 - 400 - 67 = 600 - 67 = 533.

User: Two trains are 200 km apart. One goes 80 km/h, the other 120 km/h toward each other. How soon do they meet?
Joe: Closing speed = 80 + 120 = 200 km/h. Time = 200 / 200 = 1 hour.

User: What is (2^3) * (3^2)?
Joe: 2^3 = 8, 3^2 = 9. 8 * 9 = 72.

User: A survey of 50 people finds 30 like cats and 25 like dogs, with 10 liking both. How many like neither?
Joe: At least one = 30 + 25 - 10 = 45. Neither = 50 - 45 = 5.

Joe: Shirts = 2 * 25 = 50. Socks = 3 * 4 = 12. Total = 62.

Joe: 18/24 = 3/4.

Joe: Area = 25 * 10 = 250 square meters.

Joe: 3 / 8 = 0.375 eggs per serving. 5 * 0.375 = 1.875, so you need 2 eggs.

User: You flip a coin and roll a die. What is the probability of heads and an even number?
Joe: P(heads) = 1/2, P(even) = 3/6 = 1/2. Combined = 1/2 * 1/2 = 1/4.

User: A farmer plants 15 rows of 8 trees. 10 trees die. How many are left?

User: You buy 5 pounds of flour at $0.80 per pound. What is the cost?

User: What is 225 / 15?

User: Which phone plan is cheaper: Plan A at $20/month plus 10 cents per minute, or Plan B at $35/month with unlimited minutes?
Joe: Plan A at 50 minutes costs 20 + 50 * 0.10 = 25 dollars. At 200 minutes it costs 20 + 20 = 40 dollars. Under 150 minutes Plan A is cheaper; over 150 minutes Plan B is cheaper. They break even at exactly 150 minutes.

User: A streaming service costs $12/month or $120 for a full year. How much do you save paying yearly?
Joe: Monthly costs 12 * 12 = 144 dollars per year. The yearly plan costs 120. You save 144 - 120 = 24 dollars per year.

User: A gym charges $40/month with no contract or $300 for a 12-month contract. Which is better for a year?
Joe: The monthly plan costs 40 * 12 = 480 dollars. The contract costs 300. You save 480 - 300 = 180 dollars with the contract.

User: A taxi charges a $5 base fare plus $2 per mile. Another company charges a flat $45 for any trip. After how many miles is the flat rate better?
Joe: The first taxi costs 5 + 2m. Set that equal to 45: 5 + 2m = 45, so 2m = 40, so m = 20. For trips over 20 miles the flat rate is better.

User: Bulk coffee costs $35 for a 4-pound bag. Individual bags cost $12 each for 1 pound. How much cheaper per pound buying bulk?
Joe: Bulk costs 35 / 4 = $8.75 per pound. Individual bags cost $12 per pound. Bulk saves 12 - 8.75 = $3.25 per pound.

User: Car rental A charges $50 flat. Car rental B charges $30 plus 20 cents per mile. After how many miles does B cost more than A?
Joe: B costs 30 + 0.20m. Set that equal to 50: 30 + 0.20m = 50, so 0.20m = 20, so m = 100. After 100 miles B costs more.

Joe: Job A pays 180 / 12 = $15 per hour. Job B pays 225 / 15 = $15 per hour. They pay the same hourly rate.

Joe: Plan A costs 30 / 5 = $6 per GB. Plan B costs 50 / 10 = $5 per GB. Plan B is the better value per GB.

User: Car A gets 25 miles per gallon and Car B gets 30 miles per gallon. Over 300 miles, how many fewer gallons does Car B use?

User: One gallon of paint covers 350 square feet and costs $32. Two quarts cover the same amount and cost $18 each. Which is cheaper?

User: Lawn service A charges $12 per visit, 4 visits per month. Service B charges $100 per month flat. Which is cheaper monthly?

User: Credit card A gives 1.5% cashback with no fee. Card B gives 2% cashback with a $50 annual fee. If you spend $4000/year, which gives more net cashback?
Joe: Card A gives 0.015 * 4000 = $60 with no fee, net = $60. Card B gives 0.02 * 4000 = $80 minus $50 fee = $30. Card A gives $30 more net cashback.

User: Gym membership A is $30 per month. Membership B is $250 per year. How much cheaper is B over two years?

User: Five friends eat dinner. The total bill is $135 with a 15% tip. If one friend had a $50 meal and the other four split the rest equally, what does each of the four pay?
Joe: Tip is 135 * 0.15 = $20.25, so total is 135 + 20.25 = $155.25. The remaining four split 155.25 - 50 = $105.25. Each of the four pays 105.25 / 4 = $26.31.

User: A $180 bill is split among three people. Alice pays twice as much as Bob, and Carol pays $30. How much does each pay?
Joe: Let Bob pay x, so Alice pays 2x. Then x + 2x + 30 = 180, so 3x = 150, so x = 50. Bob pays $50, Alice pays $100, and Carol pays $30.

User: A group dinner costs $210. Five people were there. Four people paid $35 each, and one person covered the rest. How much did the last person pay?
Joe: The four paid 4 * 35 = $140. The last person paid 210 - 140 = $70.

User: You and a friend split a $64 hotel room. You also owe $12 for parking. If you split the room but you pay for all the parking, what is the total each person pays?
Joe: Each pays 64 / 2 = $32 for the room. You also pay $12 for parking. You pay 32 + 12 = $44 total, and your friend pays $32.

User: Four coworkers split a $128 lunch bill. One coworker had a coupon for $18 off. If the coupon reduces the bill before splitting, what does each person pay?
Joe: The bill after the coupon is 128 - 18 = $110. Each person pays 110 / 4 = $27.50.

User: A road trip costs $120 in gas. Three friends drove different distances. Alice drove 40% of the time, Bob drove 35%, and Carol drove the rest. How much does each owe?
Joe: Carol drove 100 - 40 - 35 = 25% of the time. Alice owes 120 * 0.40 = $48, Bob owes 120 * 0.35 = $42, and Carol owes 120 * 0.25 = $30.

User: You and two friends split a $150 bill. You paid $60 already. The remaining two split the rest equally. How much does each of them pay?
Joe: After your payment, 150 - 60 = $90 remains. Each of the two pays 90 / 2 = $45.

User: A family of five splits a $200 vacation cost. Two adults each pay twice as much as each of the three children. How much does each adult and each child pay?
Joe: Let each child pay c, so each adult pays 2c. Then 2(2c) + 3c = 200, so 7c = 200, so c = 200 / 7 = about $28.57. Each child pays about $28.57 and each adult pays about $57.14.

User: Eight friends rent a cabin for $480 per week. If two friends leave after 3 days of a 7-day stay, how much should the remaining six pay to cover the full week?
Joe: Each of the eight would pay 480 / 8 = $60 for a full week. The two who left should still pay their share: 2 * 60 = $120. The remaining six each pay (480 - 120) / 6 = $60, so nothing changes.

User: A car gets 32 miles per gallon. Gas costs $4 per gallon. What is the fuel cost per mile?
Joe: Cost per mile = 4 / 32 = $0.125 per mile, or 12.5 cents per mile.

Joe: Fuel per km = 15 / 100 = 0.15 liters. Cost per km = 0.15 * 1.60 = $0.24, or 24 cents per kilometer.

User: You drive at 60 mph with a 1-second reaction time before braking. What is the stopping distance if braking distance is 30 feet at that speed?
Joe: In 1 second at 60 mph (88 feet per second) you travel 88 feet. Add 30 feet braking distance. Total stopping distance = 88 + 30 = 118 feet.

Joe: Total stopping distance = 110 + 125 = 235 feet.

User: A square room has an area of 144 square feet. What is the perimeter?

User: A box is 40 by 30 by 20 cm. A smaller box is 20 by 15 by 10 cm. How many small boxes fit in the large one?

User: You buy an item for $30 and sell it for $42. What is your profit percentage?

User: You buy an item for $20, mark it up 60%, then discount 25%. What is the selling price?
